Notes:                                                                                           附 註：
(1)    Net profit margin is profit for the year from continuing operations divided by            (1)  淨利 潤率 指 持 續 經營業 務 年內 溢 利 除 持 續 經營
       revenue from continuing operations.                                                            業 務 收 入。
(2)    Return on total assets is profit for the year from continuing operations divided          (2)  總 資 產 回報率 指 持 續 經營業 務 年內 溢 利 除 持 續
       by average total assets from continuing operations.                                            經營業 務 平均 資 產 總 額。
(3)    Return on capital employed is the ratio of profit before income tax expense               (3)  淨 資本 回 報率 指 持 續 經營業 務 利 息 及稅 項 前盈
       and interest from continuing operations divided by total assets less current                   利 除 持 續 經營業 務總 資 產 減 流 動 負債。
       liabilities from continuing operations.
(4)    Liabilities-to-Adjusted EBITDA ratio is the ratio of total liabilities from continuing    (4)     負債 對 經 調 整EBITDA倍數 指 持 續 經營業務總負
       operations divided by adjusted EBITDA.                                                            債 除 經 調 整利 息、稅 項、折 舊及 攤銷前盈利 。
(5)    Debts-to-net assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings          (5)     淨 資 產 負債率 指 計息借 貸 與 租 賃 負債 之和 除以
       and lease liabilities divided by total equity.                                                    總權 益。
(6)    Gearing ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings, and lease           (6)     資本 負債率 指 計息借 貸 與 租 賃 負債 之和 除 以 總
       liabilities divided by the sum of total equity, interest-bearing borrowings and                   權 益、計息借 貸 與 租 賃 負債 之和 。
       lease liability.
(7)    Debts-to-assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings              (7)     資 產 負債率 指 計息借 貸 與 租 賃 負債 之和 除以 總
       and lease liabilities divided by total assets.                                                    資 產。
(8)    Average finance cost is the ratio of aggregated amount of each category of                (8)     平均融 資 成 本 指 各 種 類 借 貸 成 本 乘 其佔 持 續 經
       borrowing multiply by its interest rate divided by the total borrowing from                       營業 務總 借 貸 比例，然 後 加 總。
       continuing operations.
(9)    Due to certain Group’s businesses are classified as discontinued operations               (9)     由 於二零二零 年 本 集 團 部 份 業 務 已分 類 為 已 終
       in 2020. The financial information of the Group has been restated.                                止 持 續 經營業 務 。本 集 團 的 財務 資 料已 重 列 。
(10)   The financial information of the Group has not been restated for the                      (10)    本 集 團 財 務 資 料 未 就 有意出售已終止 持 續 經營
       potential disposal of discontinued operations as the directors consider that                      業 務 進 行重 列，原 因 是 董 事 認 為 未 經 重 列 的 財
       the unrestated financial information is more appropriate for year-on-year                         務 資 料更 適 合同 比 比 較 本 集 團 業 務 營 運 的 變 化
       comparison of the change in the Group’s business operation.                                       情 況。
(11)   Core profit before income tax expense is defined as profit before income                  (11)    除 所 得 稅費 用前 核心溢 利 指 不 包 括 物 業、廠 房
       tax expense excluding impairment losses on property, plant and equipment,                         及 設 備、無 形 資 產、應 收 賬 款 及 其他 應 收 款 項
       intangible assets, accounts and other receivables and exchange gain/loss.                         減 值 虧 損 及 匯 兌 收 益 ╱虧 損 之 除所 得 稅費 用前
                                                                                                         溢利。
(12)   Core profit attributable to owners of the Company is defined as profit                    (12)    本公司 股 東 應 佔 核心溢 利 指 不 包 括 本公司 股 東
       attributable to owners of the Company excluding impairment losses on                              應 佔 之物 業、廠 房及 設 備、無 形 資 產、應 收 賬
       property, plant and equipment, intangible assets, accounts and other                              款 及 其他 應收 款 項 減值 虧 損 及 匯 兌 收 益 ╱虧 損
       receivables and exchange gain/loss attributable to owners of the Company                          及 其 有關 稅 務 影 響之本公司 股 東 應 佔 溢 利 。
       and its related tax effect.
(13)   Adjusted EBITDA is defined as profit before income tax expense from                       (13)    經調整扣除利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前盈利指不
       continuing operations, excluding impairment loss on property, plant and                           包括 物 業、廠 房及 設 備 減 值 虧 損、無 形 資 產 減
       equipment, impairment loss on intangible assets, impairment loss on accounts                      值 虧 損、應 收 賬 款 及 其他 應 收 款 項 減 值 虧 損、
       and other receivables, exchange gain/loss, interest and depreciation, depletion                   匯 兌 收 益 ╱虧 損、利 息 以 及 折 舊、損 耗 及 攤 銷
       and amortisation.                                                                                 之持 續 經營業 務 除所 得 稅費 用前 溢 利 。

It is the responsibility of the Board to ensure that the Company          董 事會 負 責確 保 本公司 之 內 部 監 控 系 統 穩
maintains sound and effective internal controls to safeguard the          健妥善而且有效，以保障股東之投資及本公
shareholders investment and the Company’s assets. The internal            司之資產。本公司之內部監控系統包含完善

FINANCIAL POSITION

The outbreak of novel coronavirus disease (COVID-19) epidemic in         於二零二零年初 爆發 新 型 冠狀 病毒
early 2020 has led to a series of precautionary and control measures     (COVID-19) 疫情已導致全球各地實施一系列
implemented across the globe and also casted downward pressure           預 防及 控 制 措 施，亦 已 對 國 內 外 能 源 價 格
on domestic and international energy prices. It may have certain         造 成 下 調 壓 力。此可能 會 對 本 集 團 之 業 務
impacts on the Group’s business operations. The Group will pay           營 運 產 生若 干 影 響。本 集 團 將 密 切 留意 疫
close attention to the development of the epidemic and assess its        情 之發 展 以 及 評 估 其 對 財 務 狀 況、現 金 流
impact on the financial position, cash flows and operating results. Up   量 及 經營業 績 之 影 響。截 至 該 等 財 務 報 表
to the date of issue of these financial statements, the assessment       刊發之日期，評估仍在進行中，且相關影響
is still in progress and the related impact could not be reasonably      在 現 階段未能作 合 理估 計。
estimated at this stage.

always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements 錯 誤 陳 述 存 在 時 總 能 發 現。 錯 誤 陳 述可以
can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, 由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨

2   BASIS OF PREPARATION (CONTINUED)                                  2   編 製 基 準（續）

    (b) Basis of preparation of the financial statements                  (b) 財 務 報表 編 製 基 準（續）
        (Continued)

        The preparation of financial statements in conformity                  編製 符合香港財務報告準則之財
        with HKFRSs requires management to make judgements,                    務報表，需要管理層作出會影響
        estimates and assumptions that affect the application                  所採用之政策及資產、負債與 收
        of policies and reported amounts of assets, liabilities,               支 之申報 金額的判斷、估 計及假
        income and expenses. The estimates and associated                      設。 估計及相關假設乃根據過往
        assumptions are based on historical experience and                     經驗及相信 於該等情況下為合理
        various other factors that are believed to be reasonable               之多個 其他因素而定，有關結果
        under the circumstances, the results of which form the                 構成未能從其他來源獲得的資產
        basis of making the judgements about carrying values                   及負債賬面值之判斷依據。實際

        from other sources. Actual results may differ from these

    (CONTINUED)

    (i)   Leased assets                                                      (i)   租賃資產

          At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                   本集團於 合約開始時評估合約是
          the contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or                 否屬於（或包含）租 賃。倘 合約為
          contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to                   換取代價而轉移已識別資產於一
          control the use of an identified asset for a period of                   段時間內之使 用控制權，則合約
          time in exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed                  屬於（或包含）租 賃。倘 客戶既有
          where the customer has both the right to direct the use                  權指示已識別資產 之使 用又有權
          of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all of               獲 取有關使用帶來之絕大部分經
          the economic benefits from that use.                                     濟 利 益，則 控 制 權已被 轉 移。

          (i)   As a lessee                                                        (i)   作為 承租 人

                Where the contract contains lease components and                         對於包含租賃部分及非租賃
                non-lease components, the Group has elected not                          部分之合約，本集團已選擇
                to separate non-lease components and accounts                            不將非租賃部分分開處理，
                for each lease component and any associated non-                         而是就所有租賃將各租賃部
                lease components as a single lease component for                         分及任何相關之非租賃部分
                all leases.                                                              作為 單一租 賃 部分入 賬。

                At the lease commencement date, the Group                                本 集 團 於 租 賃 開 始 日期 確
                recognises a right-of-use asset and a lease liability,                   認使用權資產及租賃負債，
                except for short-term leases that have a lease                           惟租期為12個月或以下之短
                term of 12 months or less and leases of low-value                        期 租 賃 或低價值 資 產租 賃
                assets. When the Group enters into a lease in                            除外。當本集團就低價值資
                respect of a low-value asset, the Group decides                          產訂立租賃時，本集團會按
                whether to capitalise the lease on a lease-by-lease                      租 賃 之個 別 情 況 決 定 是 否
                basis. The lease payments associated with those                          將租賃資本化。與該等並未
                leases which are not capitalised are recognised as                       資本化之租賃相關之租賃付
                an expense on a systematic basis over the lease                          款 於 租 期 內 有系 統 地 確 認
                term.                                                                    為開支。

              that there is no realistic prospect of recovery. This                    （部分或全部）會被撇銷。一

              repay the amounts subject to the write-off.                               來 源 以償 還 該 撇 銷 款 項。

                 total costs.                                                              進確 認。

                 The likelihood of the Group earning contractual                           在作出此等估計時，會計及

                 into account in making these estimates, such that                         蒙受合約罰款之可能性，因

                 highly probable that a significant reversal in the                        入金額不太可能出現重大撥

31 OIL PRODUCTION SHARING CONTRACT                                   31 油田 產品 分成 合 同

   Leng Jiapu Contract                                                   冷家堡合同

   Pursuant to the Leng Jiapu Contract signed in 1997, the Group         根據簽訂於一九九七年的冷家堡合同，
   agreed to acquire 70% of the production sharing interest              本集團同意收購70%之產品分成權益，
   for RMB1,008 million and to fund its share of cost of the             作 價 人 民幣1,008百萬元，並負擔 於冷
   development carried out for the realisation of oil production         家 堡 合同 所界定 之 地 區（「冷 家 堡 合同
   (the “Development Operations”) in the area as defined in the          地區」 ）內就生產石油產品（    「開發作業」   ）
   Leng Jiapu Contract (the “LJP Contract Area”), at an estimated        應 佔之 開 發 成 本，首兩 年 開 發 期 之估
   cost of US$65.5 million (approximately RMB474 million) in             計成本為65.5百萬美元（約人民幣474百
   the first two years of the development period and be further          萬 元），此 外 再負責首兩 年 後70%之 開
   responsible for 70% of the development cost after the first           發 成 本，以 取 得 冷 家 堡 合 同 地 區70%
   two years, in exchange for a 70% share in the oil production          之 產 油量。
   from the LJP Contract Area.

   Pursuant to the Leng Jiapu Contract, the Group shall bear             根 據 冷 家 堡 合同，本 集 團負擔 冷 家 堡
   70% of the costs required for the Development Operations in           合同地 區內開發 作 業 及 生 產 分成所 需
   the LJP Contract Area which shall be allocated (after deduction       之70%成本，產 量分配為（經扣除當地
   of local taxes and enterprise income tax) firstly towards             稅項及企業所得稅後）先用於收回經營
   operating costs recovery and thereafter in the proportion of          成本，其餘由本集團與中國石油按70%
   70% to the Group and 30% to PetroChina towards investment             與30%之 比例 攤 分，作 為 收 回 投 資 成
   recovery and profit.                                                  本及 賺 取 的利 潤。

   The Leng Jiapu Contract provides twenty consecutive years             冷 家 堡 合同 規 定，由開 始 商 業作 業日
   of production sharing from the date of commencement of                期 起 計 連 續 分 佔石油 產 量二十 年並 將
   commercial production and will expire on 31 December 2023.            於二零二 三年十二月三十 一日屆滿。

   In connection with the Leng Jiapu Contract, the Group has             本集團亦 就 冷 家 堡 合同與一 個 由中國
   also entered into an Entrustment Contract with an operational         石油集團全 資擁有並營 運 之 業務實體
   entity owned and operated by CNPC, whereby the latter is              訂立 委 託 合同，據 此，後者 獲 委 託 承
   entrusted to take up the responsibility as an operator. Under         擔作為作業者的責任。根據委託合同，
   the Entrustment Contract, a Joint Development Management              已成 立聯 合開 發 管 理 組 織，履行在作
   Organisation was established for the performance of the               業者 合同項 下之 責任。
   contractual responsibilities under the operatorship.

Definition:                                                            釋 義：

The petroleum reserves are classified as follows:                      石油儲 量 分 類 如下：

Proved oil and gas reserves – Proved oil and gas reserves are          探明油氣儲量 －探明油氣儲量為自給定日期
those quantities of oil and gas, which by analysis of geoscience and   至合同約定權 利 到期日（除非 有 證據 合理保
engineering data, can be estimated with reasonable certainty to be     證 該權利能夠 得到延期）    ，透 過 地 球 科 學 和
economically producible – from a given date forward, from known        工 程 數 據 的 分析，採 用 確 定 性 或 概 率性 評
reservoirs, and under existing economic conditions, operating          估，以現有經濟、作業和政府管制條件，可
methods, and government regulations – prior to the time at which       以合 理 確 定已知 油 氣 藏 經 濟 可採 油 氣 的 數
contracts providing the right to operate expire, unless evidence       量。項目須已開始萃取烴或作業者須合理確
indicates that renewal is reasonably certain, regardless of whether    定 其將於 合 理時間內開始項目。
deterministic or probabilistic methods are used for the estimation.
The project to extract the hydrocarbons must have commenced or
the operator must be reasonably certain that it will commence the
project within a reasonable time.

(i)     The areas of the reservoir considered as proved includes:             (i)     可視 為已探明油氣 藏的區 域包括：

        (A) The area identified by drilling and limited by fluid contracts,           (A)鑽 井 劃 定 及 流體介面圈定（如有）的
        if any, and (B) Adjacent undrilled portions of the reservoir that             地區，及(B)根據可用地質及工程資料可
        can, with reasonable certainty, be judged to be continuous                    合理 斷定 其可以持 續 用於 一定 經 濟 可
        with it and to contain economically producible oil or gas on                  開採 油氣的油氣 藏附 近 未鑽 探部份。
        the basis of available geoscience and engineering data.

(ii)    In the absence of data on fluid contracts, proved quantities in       (ii)    若 缺 乏 流 體 介 面 資 料，除非 地 質、工
        a reservoir are limited by the lowest known hydrocarbons (LKH)                程 或 表現資 料及可靠 技 術 確 定合 理存
        as seen in a well penetration unless geoscience, engineering,                 在 較 低 含 量，否則 油氣 藏內之探 明 儲
        or performance data and reliable technology establishes a                     量 乃根 據 油井 滲透 所 示的最 低探明含
        lower contact with reasonable certainty.                                      烴 量(LKH)而定。

(iii)   Where direct observation from well penetrations has defined a         (iii)   當透 過 井眼 直 接觀 測已確 定 最高探明
        highest known oil (HKO) elevation and the potential exists for                含油量高位(HKO)及存在潛在相關天然
        an associated gas cap, proved oil reserves may be assigned                    氣上限，則除非地質、工程或表現資料
        in the structurally higher portions of the reservoir only if                  及可靠 技 術 確 定合 理 存 在 較 高含 量，
        geoscience, engineering, or performance data and reliable                     否則 探 明 石油儲 量可分派 至 油氣 藏 構
        technology establish the higher contact with reasonable                       造 較 高的 地 區。
        certainty.

(iv)    Reserves which can be produced economically through                   (iv)    通過應用提高採收率技術（包括但不限
        application of improved recovery techniques (including, but                   於注流體）可以經濟採出的儲量包括在
        not limited to, fluid injection) are included in the proved                   探明儲 量 之列，倘：
        classification when:

        (A) Successful testing by a pilot project in an area of the                   (A) 一旦 油 氣 藏（其 物 業 不 如 整 體 油 氣
        reservoir with properties no more favourable than in the                      藏優越）的先導性試驗項目試驗 成功、
        reservoir as a whole, the operation of an installed program                   油氣 藏或 類似 油氣 藏 既定 方案作 業 成
        in the reservoir or an analogous reservoir, or other evidence                 功或使 用可靠 技 術 的其他 證據 探 明 項
        using reliable technology establishes the reasonable certainty                目和方 案 所 依 據 的工程 方法 合 理可靠
        of the engineering analysis on which the project or program                   的話；及(B)該項目已獲所有必要人士及
        was based; and (B) The project has been approved for                          實體（包括 政 府實體）批 准 進行開 發。
        development by all necessary parties and entities, including
        governmental entities.

(v)     Existing economic conditions include prices and costs                 (v)     現有的 經 濟 條 件包含確 定一 個 油氣 藏
        at which economic producibility from a reservoir is to be                     經 濟 生 產 能力的 價格和 成 本。 除非由
        determined. The price shall be the average price during the                   合同約定，該 價 格 是 指 在 本 報 告期 截
        12-month period prior to the ending date of the period covered                止日以前的十二個月的算術平均價格，
        by the report, determined as an unweighted arithmetic average                 乃 確 定 為 該 期 間內每 個月第一 天 價格
        of the first-day-of-the-month price for each month within such                的 未 加 權 算術平均 數，但不 包括 基 於
        period, unless prices are defined by contractual arrangements,                未 來條 件 做出的 價格調 整。
        excluding escalations based upon future conditions.

Developed oil and gas reserves – Developed oil and gas reserves               已開 發 油 氣 儲 量 －已 開 發 油 氣 儲 量 為 預 期
are reserves of any category that can be expected to be recovered:            可透 過以下方 式開 採 的 儲 量：

(i)     Through existing wells with existing equipment and operating          (i)     利 用現有 設 備 和 作 業 方法，或者開 採
        methods or in which the cost of the required equipment is                     儲 量 所需的開發設備 成 本明顯低 於 鑽
        relatively minor compared to the cost of a new well; and                      探 一口新井 所 需 成 本，可從 現 有 油 氣
                                                                                      井中進行開 採 的 儲 量；及

(ii)    Through installed extraction equipment and infrastructure             (ii)    當通 過 除油氣井開採 外的其他方 式 進
        operational at the time of the reserves estimate if the extraction            行 開 採，利 用儲 量 估 計 時 點已安 裝的
        is by means not involving a well.                                             開採 設備 和基 礎設 施可開採的儲 量。

Undeveloped oil and gas reserves – Undeveloped oil and gas                    未開發油氣儲量 – 未開發油氣儲 量指預 期可
reserves are reserves of any category that are expected to be                 從未鑽區域的新井中採出，或再完井需要較
recovered from new wells on undrilled acreage, or from existing wells         高支出的 現有井中採 出的 儲 量。
where a relatively major expenditure is required from recompletion.

(i)     Reserves on undrilled acreage shall be limited to those directly      (i)     未 鑽區域的儲 量僅 限 於直 接扣 除開發
        offsetting development spacing areas that are reasonably                      間距區域，有一定把握在鑽後能採油，
        certain of production when drilled, unless evidence using                     除非 證 實 運 用可靠 技 術 能有一定 把握
        reliable technology exists that establishes reasonable certainty              在 更 遠 距 離 在 經 濟上可生 產。
        of economic producibility at greater distances.

(ii)    Undrilled locations can be classified as having undeveloped           (ii)    倘已採 納 的 開 發方 案表明 計 劃 在 五年
        reserves only if a development plan has been adopted                          內（在 特 定 環 境 下 須 較 長 時 間 者 除 外）
        indicating that they are scheduled to be drilled within five years,           鑽 井，未 鑽 區 域 方可分 類 為 擁 有未 開
        unless the specific circumstances, justify a longer time.                     發 儲 量。

(iii)   Under no circumstances shall estimates for undeveloped                (iii)   任 何未開發 儲 量的估 計 均不 得包括擬
        reserves be attributable to any acreage for which an                          運 用流體 注 入或其他 改 良 採 油技 術的
        application of fluid injection or other improved recovery                     任 何 區 域，除非 該等 技 術已通 過 在相
        technique is contemplated, unless such techniques have been                   同 油 藏 或 類 似 油 藏 的 實 際項目，或 通
        proved effective by actual projects in the same reservoir or                  過 採 用可靠 技 術 證 實有一定 把握 的 其
        an analogous reservoir, or by other evidence using reliable                   他 證據 證 明為 有 效。
        technology establishing reasonable certainty.
